def GlobalTheory.EX.TypeIProduction.TypeIAtOriginalWordWithRow : {eps : Real} →
  (F : Formalism.MainNotions.FamP eps) →
    (x : Real) →
      @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.x1 eps (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F))
          x →
        (i : @Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.Ix eps (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F) x) →
          @Formalism.BasicNotions.Rows.Data.Word
              (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.D eps (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F) x i).R
              (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.D eps (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F) x i).what₂ →
            Prop :=
fun {eps} F x hx i u =>
  let D := @Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.D eps (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F) x i;
  let L := @Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.L eps (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F) x i;
  let X := @Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.Gx eps F x i;
  let Y := @Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.Gy eps F x i;
  let K := @Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.GK eps F;
  let ex := @Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.ex eps F x hx i;
  have Z :=
    @HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHPow.{0, 0} Real Real Real.instPow)
      (@Formalism.BasicNotions.SizeData₂.sc
          (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.D eps (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F) x i) L).X'
      (@HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHDiv.{0} Real (@DivInvMonoid.toDiv.{0} Real Real.instDivInvMonoid))
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 3)
          (@instOfNatAtLeastTwo.{0} Real (nat_lit 3) Real.instNatCast TypeIAtOriginalWord._proof_1))
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 4)
          (@instOfNatAtLeastTwo.{0} Real (nat_lit 4) Real.instNatCast TypeIAtOriginalWord._proof_2)));
  ∃ p,
    And (Formalism.MainNotions.G.Cand D L K p)
      (And
        (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE
          ((@Formalism.BasicNotions.SizeData₂.sc
                (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.D eps (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F) x i) L).p0
            D.R.k)
          (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast p))
        (And (@LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT Z (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast p))

              (@HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHMul.{0} Real Real.instMul)
                (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 2)
                  (@instOfNatAtLeastTwo.{0} Real (nat_lit 2) Real.instNatCast TypeIAtOriginalWord._proof_3))
                Z))
            (And
              (p ∉
                @Formalism.BasicNotions.SizeData₂.drop
                  (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.D eps (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F) x i) L)
              (∃ w,

def GlobalTheory.EX.TypeIProduction.TypeIProductionConclusion : {N : Formalism.MainNotions.FamClass → Prop} →
  {DR : GlobalTheory.Fc.R1.DefiniteSideType} → GlobalTheory.Fc.HypsAlt N DR → Real → UpperTheory.Top.FReq → Prop :=
fun {N} {DR} H la Q =>
  ∃ eps₀,
    And (@LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) eps₀)
      (∀ (eps : Real),
        @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) eps →
          @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE eps eps₀ →
            @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT eps
                (@HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real
                  (@instHDiv.{0} Real (@DivInvMonoid.toDiv.{0} Real Real.instDivInvMonoid))
                  (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 1) (@One.toOfNat1.{0} Real Real.instOne))
                  (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 52)
                    (@instOfNatAtLeastTwo.{0} Real (nat_lit 52) Real.instNatCast TypeIProductionConclusion._proof_1))) →
              ∀ (K : Formalism.MainNotions.TransportedAlt),
                ∃ F CH,
                  And (@GlobalTheory.Fc.HypsAlt.𝒜 N DR H eps F)
                    (And (@UpperTheory.Top.FReq.cls Q la eps F)
                      (And
                        (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE
                          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) CH)
                        (And (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.Large eps F K CH)
                          (And
                            (@Formalism.MainNotions.ChosenE eps F
                              (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 3)
                                (@instOfNatAtLeastTwo.{0} Real (nat_lit 3) Real.instNatCast
                                  TypeIProductionConclusion._proof_2))
                              CH)
                            (And (@Formalism.MainNotions.TuSize eps F)
                              (And (@Formalism.MainNotions.DGamSize eps F)
                                (∀ (y : Real),
                                  ∃ x,
                                    ∃ (_ : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE y x) (hx :
                                      @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT
                                        (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.x1 eps
                                          (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F))
                                        x)
                                      (_ :
                                      @Membership.mem.{0, 0} Real (Set.{0} Real) (@Set.instMembership.{0} Real)
                                        (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamCore.Win eps
                                          (@Formalism.MainNotions.FamP.toFamCore eps F))
                                        x),
                                      ∃ i u, @TypeIAtOriginalWord eps F x hx i u))))))))

  {DR : GlobalTheory.Fc.R1.DefiniteSideType} → GlobalTheory.Fc.HypsAlt N DR → Real → UpperTheory.Top.FReq → Prop :=
fun {N} {DR} H la Q =>
  ∃ eps₀,
    And (@LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) eps₀)

                (@HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real

                  (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 1) (@One.toOfNat1.{0} Real Real.instOne))
                  (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 52)
                    (@instOfNatAtLeastTwo.{0} Real (nat_lit 52) Real.instNatCast TypeIProductionConclusion._proof_1))) →
              ∀ (K : Formalism.MainNotions.TransportedAlt),
                ∃ F CH,

                      (And
                        (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE

def GlobalTheory.EX.TypeIProduction.FirstScalarLayerSurjection.{u_1, u_2} : {O : Type u_1} →
  {A : Type u_2} →
    [inst : CommRing.{u_1} O] →
      [inst_1 : AddCommGroup.{u_2} A] →
        [inst_2 :
            @Module.{u_1, u_2} O A (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} O (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_1} O inst))
              (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} A inst_1)] →
          O →
            Nat →
              @Submodule.{u_1, u_2} O A (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} O (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_1} O inst))
                  (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} A inst_1) inst_2 →
                @LinearMap.{u_1, u_1, u_2, u_1} O O
                    (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} O (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_1} O inst))
                    (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} O (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_1} O inst))
                    (@RingHom.id.{u_1} O
                      (@Semiring.toNonAssocSemiring.{u_1} O
                        (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} O (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_1} O inst))))
                    A O (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} A inst_1)
                    (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_1} O
                      (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} O (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_1} O inst)))
                    inst_2
                    (@Semiring.toModule.{u_1} O
                      (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} O (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_1} O inst))) →
                  Prop :=
fun {O} {A} [inst : ⋯] [inst_1 : ⋯] [inst_2 : ⋯] p T U row =>

    @HSMul.hSMul.{u_1, u_2, u_2} O
      (@Submodule.{u_1, u_2} O A (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} O (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_1} O inst))
        (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} A inst_1) inst_2)
